00238:     if not p:

00240:     return [o for o in p.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH']

00242: def texture_material(mat,kind):

00244:     nt=mat.node_tree

00262:     nt.links.new(tex.outputs['Fac'],ramp.inputs[0])
00263:     nt.links.new(ramp.outputs[0],bs.inputs['Base Color'])

00081: def ring(parent,name,center,radius,tube=.025,col=iron,segments=20):
00082:     x,y,z = center
00083:     for k in range(segments):
00084:         a = 2*math.pi*k/segments
00085:         b = 2*math.pi*(k+1)/segments
00086:         R(parent,name+'_rim_%d'%k,
00087:           (x,y+radius*math.cos(a),z+radius*math.sin(a)),
00088:           (x,y+radius*math.cos(b),z+radius*math.sin(b)),tube,col)

00090: def wheel(parent,name,center,radius):
00091:     x,y,z = center
00092:     ring(parent,name,center,radius,.034,worniron)
00093:     ring(parent,name+'_inner',(x+.025,y,z),radius*.86,.015,iron,16)
00094:     R(parent,name+'_hub',(x-.09,y,z),(x+.11,y,z),radius*.15,worniron)
00095:     for k in range(5):
00096:         a = k*2*math.pi/5+.2
00097:         p = (x,y+radius*.16*math.cos(a),z+radius*.16*math.sin(a))
00098:         q = (x,y+radius*.56*math.cos(a+.35),z+radius*.56*math.sin(a+.35))
00099:         t = (x,y+radius*.93*math.cos(a+.16),z+radius*.93*math.sin(a+.16))
00100:         R(parent,name+'_spoke_a%d'%k,p,q,.034,iron)
00101:         R(parent,name+'_spoke_b%d'%k,q,t,.030,iron)
